These profiles establish the P9a source-input and P9b bounded original call/reference contracts. Anonymous closure/async declarations retain original ownership. Every certified source call/reference has a unique original target, empty remaining conditions and checked proof; unsupported dispatch keeps explicit outcomes. mini-redis keeps resolver 1 build/dev/platform feature-unification gaps and original macro attributes. env_logger preserves workspace inheritance, local env_filter dependencies, selected source features and generated/scoped item boundaries. Source winners require an original accessible declaration with no remaining import conditions. Binary crates keep external identities without synthesized exports. Counts are compilation-scoped: a source file can occur in independent library, binary and example invocations.

Features/defaults and the x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu triple/cfg values are recorded qualification assumptions, never derived from the host or rustc. Unreviewed Cargo configuration, unavailable compiler inputs, generated modules, proc macros and hygiene retain gaps. Literal Cargo aliases and registry Rust-version selection preserve original source bindings while binary dependency selection stays unavailable. No Cargo/rustc, build script, registry/Git dependency, target configuration or macro executes. Upstream inputs are neither edited nor replaced. These are bounded source integration counts. Trait/generic dispatch, borrowing and arbitrary receiver coercion, generated items, routers, runtime behavior, native operating systems, performance and broad production recall remain P9c–P9e/P10 work.

The scripts check HEAD and tracked-file differences, copy regular source/manifest/lock/ignore/config blobs directly from the pinned trees, retain SHA-256 path/mode/byte digests, validate original ranges and edge evidence, and compare complete graph fingerprints. Reproduce with exact checkouts: node scripts/qualify-rust-sources.mjs --mini-redis /path/to/mini-redis --env-logger /path/to/env_logger. Roadmap.
